## AgentCenter Nuxt: Skills Homepage Structure Documentation

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here is the complete documentation of the current homepage structure for the Skills marketplace:

---

### File Paths

**Main Pages:**
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/index.vue` — Root homepage
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — Browse/list page (shared across all extension categories: Skills, MCP, slash commands, plugins)

**No dedicated "Skills-only" homepage** — The app uses a unified extensions list with category filtering (via query params like `?category=skills`). Skills is one of four categories defined in the type system.

---

### Extension Data Model

**Available Fields** (from `/shared/db/queries-types.ts` > `ExtensionListItem`):
```
id, slug, category, badge, scope, funcCat, subCat, l2, deptId
iconEmoji, iconColor, name, nameZh, tagline, taglineZh
description, descriptionZh, downloadsCount, starsAvg, tagIds
```

**Categories**: `"skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"`  
**Badges**: `"official" | "popular" | "new"` (or null)  
**Scope**: `"personal" | "org" | "enterprise"`

---

### Homepage Composition (Current State as of 2026-05-21)

The homepage at `/app/pages/index.vue` currently contains **three main sections** (in order):

#### 1. **Featured Spotlight Hero** (ExtFeatureHero.vue)
- Displays one curated "Featured this week" extension
- Components: `/app/components/extension/ExtFeatureHero.vue`
- Layout: Left column (name, icon, tagline, description, stats), right column (install command, "View extension" link)
- Features:
  - Large serif title (40px on desktop, 34px mobile)
  - Icon emoji (22px) in a colored rounded square
  - Tagline in italic serif
  - Description paragraph
  - Stats line: star rating, download count, department trail
  - InstallCommand (copyable CLI snippet)
  - Install button (accent-filled CTA, large)
  - "View extension" link (accent text, secondary)
- Border: Left-side 2px accent color bar
- Fallback: If no extension is marked featured, renders an editorial card with "AgentCenter" heading

#### 2. **MCP Panorama Feature Card** (McpPanoramaFeature.vue)
- Teaser card promoting `/mcp-panorama` page
- Path: `/app/components/extension/McpPanoramaFeature.vue`
- Layout: Left column (eyebrow "MCP PANORAMA", title, description, stats), right column (decorative 6×4 grid motif, hidden <640px)
- Accent text CTA with arrow icon

#### 3. **Trending Section**
- Header: "Trending" title + "Browse all {count}" link
- Grid of ExtCard components (8 items maximum, from sorted list)
- Path: `/app/components/extension/ExtGrid.vue`

**Why no category filtering on home?** The homepage displays *all extension categories* as a trending mix. Type-first browsing happens at `/extensions` when users apply category filters.

---

### Browse/List Page (`/extensions`) Composition

The `/app/pages/extensions/index.vue` page contains:

1. **Header** — "Browse all" title + optional "View the MCP Panorama" link (shown only when `?category=mcp`)
2. **FilterBar** — Compact single-row filter rail (revised 2026-05-21 per decision #3)
   - Contains: ScopePills, DeptPicker, CreatorPicker, PublisherPicker, TagDrawer
   - Path: `/app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue`
3. **ResultsSummary** — Result count chip + active filter chips (dismissible) + Sort select
   - Path: `/app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue`
   - Shows: "Showing {count}", each active filter as a removable pill, "Clear all" link
4. **ExtGrid or ExtGridSkeleton** — Responsive card grid
   - Grid: `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(320px,1fr))] gap-4`
   - Cards collapse to single column below 640px wide
   - Empty state with SearchX icon, heading, hint text, and optional "Clear filters" link

---

### Component Architecture

**Hero & Featured:**
- **ExtFeatureHero.vue** — Featured extension on homepage (full-width, large title, two-column layout, accent border)
- **ExtHero.vue** — Detail page header (compact variant: smaller layout, used on `/extensions/[slug]`)
- **McpPanoramaFeature.vue** — MCP Panorama teaser (homepage, decorative motif grid)

**Card Components:**
- **ExtCard.vue** — Browse/list card (icon, name + badge, 3-line description, meta line with dept/stars/downloads)
  - Single meta line format (12px mono): `[department]` · `[star rating]` · `[download count]`
  - Hover state: `-translate-y-0.5` lift, subtle border tint
  - Whole card clickable (after pseudo-element overlay link)
  - Save/Install/Share stay independently clickable
- **ExtGrid.vue** — Responsive container (auto-fill grid layout, empty state handling)

**Filter Components** (`/app/components/filters/`):
- **ScopePills.vue** — Scope toggle (All, Personal, Org, Enterprise) — inline pills
- **DeptPicker.vue** — Department dropdown with hierarchy
- **CreatorPicker.vue** — Creator/author dropdown
- **PublisherPicker.vue** — Organization dropdown
- **TagDrawer.vue** — Tag multi-select with "any/all" toggle
- **SortSelect.vue** — Sort by (Downloads, Stars, Recently Added)
- **FilterBar.vue** — Container wrapping all the above in a single flexbox row

**Metadata & Summary:**
- **ResultsSummary.vue** — Count display + active filter chips + Sort control
- **InstallCommand.vue** — Copyable CLI command snippet
- **InstallButton.vue**, **SaveButton.vue**, **ShareButton.vue** — Individual action buttons

---

### i18n / Locales

**Translation Keys Live In:**
- `/i18n/locales/en.json` (English)
- `/i18n/locales/zh.json` (Chinese)

**Relevant Keys for Homepage:**
```
home:
  featuredLabel: "Featured this week"
  featuredDescription: "Marketplace for AI agent extensions…"
  trendingTitle: "Trending"
  browseAll: "Browse all {count}"
  viewExtension: "View extension"

extensions:
  browseTitle: "Browse all"
  mcpPanoramaFeature: {eyebrow, title, description, stats, cta}

extensions.empty: {noResultsFor, noResultsFiltered, noExtensionsYet, hintAdjust, hintCheckBack}
extensions.summary: {showing, showingZero, clearAll, removeFilter}

sidebar:
  skills: "Skills"
```

**URLs Always Locale-Prefixed:**
- `/en/` and `/zh/` (strategy: 'prefix')
- Extension fields support dual-language values (nameZh, descriptionZh, taglineZh)

---

### Theme Tokens & Constraints

**From `/app/assets/css/tailwind.css` and CLAUDE.md decision #11:**

**Accent Usage Rule (Decision #11):**
> `--color-accent` is reserved for primary CTAs, focus rings, the **home featured hero**, and the active extension-detail install action. Secondary active states (sidebar nav, filter trigger pills, non-primary picker chips) use **neutral ink + left-rule or bold weight**, not tinted fills. The home should carry at most **two accent moments per viewport**; the goal is "one intentional accent moment" wherever practical.

**Current Accent Moments on Homepage:**
1. Featured hero — 2px left border + "View extension" link (accent text)
2. MCP Panorama CTA — "Open the panorama" link (accent text)
3. Browse all link in trending header — (accent text)

**Theme Tokens (Tailwind CSS):**
```
Ivory (Default):
  --color-accent: oklch(40% 0.09 35)          // Warm burnt orange
  --color-accent-fg: oklch(98% 0.005 70)      // Nearly white
  --color-card: #ffffff                        // Pure white
  --color-border: oklch(92% 0.012 70)         // Very light grey
  --color-ink: oklch(22% 0.02 60)             // Very dark grey
  --color-ink-muted: oklch(48% 0.015 60)      // Medium grey

Dark:
  --color-accent: oklch(72% 0.18 200)         // Cyan/blue
  --color-card: oklch(19% 0.008 240)          // Dark navy
  --color-border: oklch(24% 0.008 240)        // Dark grey
  --color-ink: oklch(90% 0.006 240)           // Off-white

Mono:
  --color-accent: oklch(22% 0 0)              // Black
  --color-accent-fg: #ffffff                  // White
  All colors: pure greyscale (chroma: 0)
```

**Fonts:**
- Display: `--font-serif: "Fraunces"` (serif for headings, titles, tags)
- UI: `--font-sans: "Inter"` (sans-serif for body, buttons, labels)
- Monospace: `--font-mono: "JetBrains Mono"` (counts, code, stats)

**Card Radius:**
- `--radius-card: 10px`

---

### Recent Refinements (Daily Log Entries)

**2026-05-21** — "ext-list-elegance" PR #27:
- **SimplifiedExtCard** — Removed inline tag pills and Install button; now a preview rectangle with icon, name, description (3 lines), and single meta line
- **Quieter filter rail** — Collapsed from 3 rows to single row; removed labels and quick-filter chips (Trending/New/Official/Free)
- **ResultsSummary strip** — New component showing count + dismissible filter chips + sort control
- **Extended filter state** — Active filters render as removable pills between filters and grid
- **Detail-page improvements** — Larger serif title, stronger icon, more confident primary Install CTA, tab bodies in framed cards, underline-on-active tabs, enriched Related sidebar (badge + dept + stars)

**2026-05-19** — Multi-MCP tool support:
- Tools can now expose multiple MCP servers
- Tool-as-card layout with MCP pills inside
- Every count clickable for drill/filter actions

**2026-05-18** — Type-first browsing + Featured hero:
- Explore dropdown lists four types (Skills, MCP, Slash Commands, Plugins) + MCP Panorama
- Featured extension on homepage (real curated extension, not placeholder)
- Mono Clean theme added (three-way theme switcher)
- Sidebar leads with function-types taxonomy tree
- Quieter editorial shell (no dividing lines, borderless search, accent reserved for hero + CTAs)

---

### API Endpoints

**Data Sources (server-side, called from pages):**
- `/api/internal/featured` → returns `{ featured: ExtensionListItem | null }`
- `/api/internal/extensions` → returns `{ items: ExtensionListItem[], total: number, filters: {} }`
  - Query params: `sort`, `category`, `scope`, `dept`, `creator`, `publisher`, `tags`, `tagMatch`, `q`, `page`
- `/api/internal/facets` → returns `{ creators: CreatorFacet[], publishers: PublisherFacet[], tags: TagFacet[] }`
- `/api/internal/mcp-landscape` → returns MCP Panorama data

---

### Summary Table

| Aspect | Details |
|--------|---------|
| **Homepage Route** | `/app/pages/index.vue` (locale-prefixed: `/en/`, `/zh/`) |
| **Browse Route** | `/app/pages/extensions/index.vue` (all categories, category-filtered via ?category query) |
| **Featured Component** | `ExtFeatureHero.vue` (curated spotlight with large hero, stats, install command) |
| **List Component** | `ExtCard.vue` (preview rectangles: icon, name, description, meta line) |
| **Grid Container** | `ExtGrid.vue` (responsive auto-fill, collapses to 1 column <640px) |
| **Filter Rail** | `FilterBar.vue` + 5 filter components (single-row layout, quiet pills) |
| **Active Filters** | `ResultsSummary.vue` (removable chips, count, sort control) |
| **Theme Constraint** | Max 2 accent moments per viewport (decision #11); secondary states use weight/rules, not tint |
| **Fonts** | Fraunces (display), Inter (UI), JetBrains Mono (counts) |
| **Bilingual** | All UI + content supports EN/ZH; URLs always locale-prefixed |
| **Latest Refine** | 2026-05-21: Simplified cards, quieter filters, dismissible filter chips, more confident detail hero |

---

This documentation captures the current state as of the last commit (2026-05-21). The homepage does not filter by "Skills" by default — it shows trending items across all categories. Type-first navigation happens on the Browse page via the Explore dropdown or query params.

    // 微信设置字体最大，布局乱的补丁

      return ua.match(/MicroMessenger/i) == "micromessenger";
    }
    if (is_weixn()) {
      if (
        typeof WeixinJSBridge == "object" &&
        typeof WeixinJSBridge.invoke == "function"
      ) {
        handleFontSize();
      } else {
        if (document.addEventListener) {
          document.addEventListener("WeixinJSBridgeReady", handleFontSize, false);
        } else if (document.attachEvent) {
          document.attachEvent("WeixinJSBridgeReady", handleFontSize);
          document.attachEvent("onWeixinJSBridgeReady", handleFontSize);

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Building2, Download, Star } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	import { deptPath, MY_DEPT_ID } from "~~/shared/data/departments"
4	import type { ExtensionListItem } from "~~/shared/db/queries-types"
5	import type { Locale } from "~~/shared/types"
6	
7	const props = defineProps<{ ext: ExtensionListItem }>()
8	
9	const { t, locale } = useI18n()
10	const localePath = useLocalePath()
11	
12	const BADGE_CLASS = {
13	  official: "badge-official",
14	  popular: "badge-popular",
15	  new: "badge-new",
16	} as const
17	
18	const name = computed(() =>
19	  locale.value === "zh" && props.ext.nameZh ? props.ext.nameZh : props.ext.name,
20	)
21	const desc = computed(() =>
22	  locale.value === "zh" && props.ext.descriptionZh
23	    ? props.ext.descriptionZh
24	    : props.ext.description,
25	)
26	
27	const deptTrail = computed(() =>
28	  props.ext.deptId ? deptPath(props.ext.deptId, locale.value as Locale) : null,
29	)
30	const deptLeaf = computed(() =>
31	  deptTrail.value && deptTrail.value.length > 0
32	    ? deptTrail.value[deptTrail.value.length - 1]
33	    : null,
34	)
35	const isMine = computed(() => {
36	  const id = props.ext.deptId
37	  if (!id) return false
38	  return id === MY_DEPT_ID || id.startsWith(`${MY_DEPT_ID}.`)
39	})
40	
41	function formatCount(n: number): string {
42	  if (n < 1000) return String(n)
43	  return `${(n / 1000).toFixed(n >= 100000 ? 0 : 1)}k`
44	}
45	
46	const ratingLabel = computed(() => {
47	  const raw = props.ext.starsAvg
48	  if (raw === null || raw === undefined || raw === "") return null
49	  const n = Number(raw)
50	  if (Number.isNaN(n)) return null
51	  return n.toFixed(1)
52	})
53	</script>
54	
55	<template>
56	  <article
57	    class="group relative flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-(--radius-card) border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) p-5 transition-all duration-200 ease-out hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-(--color-accent)/30 hover:shadow-[0_2px_10px_-4px_oklch(0_0_0_/_0.08)]"
58	  >
59	    <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
60	      <div
61	        class="flex size-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-[8px] border-[1.5px] text-[18px]"
62	        :style="{
63	          background: `${ext.iconColor ?? '#888'}1c`,
64	          borderColor: `${ext.iconColor ?? '#888'}33`,
65	        }"
66	      >
67	        {{ ext.iconEmoji }}
68	      </div>
69	      <div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-1.5">
70	        <NuxtLink
71	          :to="localePath(`/extensions/${ext.slug}`)"
72	          class="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold tracking-tight transition-colors hover:text-(--color-accent) after:absolute after:inset-0 after:content-['']"
73	        >
74	          {{ name }}

79	          :class="BADGE_CLASS[ext.badge]"
80	        >
81	          {{ t(`extensions.badges.${ext.badge}`) }}
82	        </span>
83	      </div>
84	    </div>
85	
86	    <p
87	      v-if="desc"
88	      class="text-(--color-ink-muted) text-[13px] leading-[1.55] overflow-hidden"
89	      style="display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 3; -webkit-box-orient: vertical;"
90	    >
91	      {{ desc }}
92	    </p>

99	      >

101	        <span class="truncate font-semibold">{{ deptLeaf }}</span>
102	      </span>

107	          <span class="font-semibold text-(--color-ink)">{{ ratingLabel }}</span>

1	import * as extensionsRepo from "~~/server/repositories/extensions"
2	import {
3	  parseFilters,
4	  searchParamsToInput,
5	} from "~~/shared/validators/filters"
6	
7	export default defineEventHandler(async (event) => {
8	  const url = getRequestURL(event)
9	  const filters = parseFilters(searchParamsToInput(url.searchParams))
10	
11	  try {
12	    const db = useDb()
13	    const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
14	      extensionsRepo.findManyForList(db, filters),
15	      extensionsRepo.countFiltered(db, filters),
16	    ])
17	    return { items, total, filters }
18	  } catch (err) {
19	    console.error("[api/internal/extensions] db error:", err)
20	    throw createError({
21	      statusCode: 500,
22	      statusMessage: "Failed to load extensions",
23	    })
24	  }
25	})
26

6	  type PublisherFacet,

8	} from "~~/server/utils/queries/facets"
9	
10	export default defineEventHandler(async (): Promise<{
11	  creators: CreatorFacet[]
12	  publishers: PublisherFacet[]
13	  tags: TagFacet[]
14	}> => {

16	    const [creators, publishers, tags] = await Promise.all([
17	      listPublishedCreators(),

19	      listTagsWithCounts(),
20	    ])

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/i-don-t-like-the-majestic-glade.md",
  "content": "# Redesign the home as a Skills-focused, ranked discovery page\n\n## Context\n\nThe current home (`app/pages/index.vue`) is a multi-category surface: a curated **Featured this week** hero (`ExtFeatureHero`), an **MCP Panorama** teaser (`McpPanoramaFeature`), and an 8-tile **Trending** grid that mixes all four extension categories (Skills/MCP/Slash/Plugins).\n\nThe user wants to model the home after [skillhub.cn](https://skillhub.cn), which positions itself as \"an AI Skills community for Chinese users, curating Top 50 high-quality AI Skills with security review and multi-dimensional evaluation.\" The site is fully client-rendered, so its visual layout couldn't be inspected — the plan is built from its **stated concept** plus the four reshape signals the user explicitly confirmed:\n\n1. **Top-N ranked leaderboard** (numbered list, not a grid)\n2. **Skills-only home** (MCP / Slash / Plugins move off the home; still browsable at `/extensions`)\n3. **Big hero with prominent search** (centered search input is the headline interaction)\n4. **Category / tag rail on the home** (top tags surfaced inline, not only on `/extensions`)\n5. **Drop both Featured hero and MCP Panorama** from the home — fresh layout\n\n> If the screenshot arrives before implementation starts, refine the chip-rail content (taxonomy vs. tag facets), the rank-row composition, and any hero ornamentation accordingly. The structural skeleton below should still hold.\n\n## New home composition (top → bottom)\n\n```\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  HomeHero                                                     │\n│   • eyebrow: \"AGENTCENTER · SKILLS\"                           │\n│   • h1 (Fraunces, large): editorial title                     │\n│   • subtitle: one-line value prop                             │\n│   • <search input> centered, full width, with submit icon     │\n│   • optional secondary \"Browse all skills →\" link             │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  HomeTagRail (horizontal, single row, overflow scroll on sm)  │\n│   • Top ~8 tags from `/api/internal/facets` sorted by count   │\n│   • Each chip → /extensions?category=skills&tags=<id>         │\n│   • \"All skills\" chip first → /extensions?category=skills     │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│  ExtRankedList — \"Top 50 Skills\"                              │\n│   • Header: title + sort hint (\"by installs · weekly\")        │\n│   • 50 numbered rows (1–50), single column                    │\n│   • Row layout (desktop):                                      │\n│       ┌────┬──────┬──────────────────────────────┬─────────┐  │\n│       │ 01 │ icon │ name + badge / 1-line desc /  │ Install │  │\n│       │    │      │ meta (dept · ★ · ↓)           │ button  │  │\n│       └────┴──────┴──────────────────────────────┴─────────┘  │\n│   • Whole row clickable → detail; Install stays clickable     │\n│   • Mobile: stack meta below name; Install becomes icon       │\n│   • Footer: \"View all skills →\" link                          │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nDecision #11 (accent budget): at most two accent moments per viewport. The hero's search submit button is accent #1. Install buttons in the ranked list are not all accent — only the top-3 rows get the accent fill; rows 4–50 use the neutral outline `InstallButton` variant. The \"View all skills\" footer link is plain ink, not accent.\n\n## Files to modify / add\n\n**Modify**\n- `app/pages/index.vue` — replace entire `<template>` and fetch logic. Use `/api/internal/extensions?category=skills&sort=downloads` and `/api/internal/facets`. Slice `items.slice(0, 50)` for the ranked list. Drop `ExtFeatureHero`, `McpPanoramaFeature` imports and the fallback editorial card. Keep `defineOgImageComponent` (update title/desc to Skills framing).\n- `i18n/locales/en.json` and `i18n/locales/zh.json` — `home` block:\n  - **Remove** `featuredLabel`, `featuredDescription`, `trendingTitle`, `browseAll`, `viewExtension`, `installsCount`.\n  - **Add** `heroEyebrow`, `heroTitle`, `heroSubtitle`, `heroSearchPlaceholder`, `heroSearchSubmit`, `heroBrowseAll`, `tagRailAll`, `topListTitle`, `topListSubtitle`, `topListViewAll`, `rankAriaLabel`.\n\n**Add**\n- `app/components/home/HomeHero.vue` — hero with eyebrow, h1, subtitle, search input. On submit/Enter: `useLocalePath()` → push `/extensions?q=<encoded>&category=skills`. Use existing search icon (Lucide `Search`). Tailwind, single accent submit pill.\n- `app/components/home/HomeTagRail.vue` — receives `tags: TagFacet[]` prop. Renders an \"All skills\" chip first, then the top 8 by `count`. Localized via `labelEn`/`labelZh`. Style mirrors the existing `filters/ScopePills.vue` chip (neutral ink, bordered, no tint), per decision #11.\n- `app/components/extension/ExtRankedList.vue` — receives `items: ExtensionListItem[]` (already top-50). Renders a numbered `<ol>` of rows. Row internals reuse the icon swatch styling from `ExtCard.vue` (lines 60–68), the dept-leaf + stars + downloads meta line (lines 94–115), and the `BADGE_CLASS` map. Top-3 rows get the accent install variant; rows 4+ use the neutral one. Number column uses `font-mono` JetBrains Mono per decision #11 (mono for counts).\n\n**Do not touch** (used elsewhere)\n- `ExtFeatureHero.vue`, `McpPanoramaFeature.vue` — no longer imported by home, but keep for now; removing dead files is out of scope for this PR.\n- `ExtCard.vue`, `ExtGrid.vue` — still drive `/extensions`.\n- `FilterBar.vue` and children — not touched; `/extensions` is unchanged.\n\n## Reuse map (do not reinvent)\n\n- **Icon swatch**: copy the inline-style block from `ExtCard.vue:60-68` (size-11 rounded, `iconColor` bg/border).\n- **Badge styling**: reuse `BADGE_CLASS` map and `extensions.badges.*` i18n keys (`ExtCard.vue:12-16`, `i18n/locales/en.json:97-101`).\n- **Meta line**: dept leaf + rating + downloads + `formatCount()` from `ExtCard.vue:41-115`. Extract `formatCount` into a small composable `app/composables/useFormatCount.ts` if it's needed in both components (current usage is just `ExtCard.vue`; can also copy verbatim, judgment call at implementation time).\n- **Install action**: reuse `InstallButton.vue`. If it doesn't already have a `variant: \"accent\" | \"outline\"` prop, add one — that's a one-line internal change, not a public API change.\n- **Locale-aware name/desc**: reuse the `locale.value === \"zh\" && ext.nameZh ? ext.nameZh : ext.name` idiom (`ExtCard.vue:18-25`).\n- **Tag labels**: existing `TagFacet` carries `labelEn`/`labelZh`; render the locale-correct one.\n- **Search submit**: navigate via `useLocalePath()` to keep the locale prefix (decision #5: locale URLs always prefixed).\n\n## Data flow\n\n```\nuseFetch /api/internal/extensions ?category=skills&sort=downloads\n  → data.items.slice(0, 50)  → ExtRankedList\n  → data.total               → footer \"View all {n} skills\"\n\nuseFetch /api/internal/facets\n  → data.tags\n      .sort((a,b) => b.count - a.count)\n      .slice(0, 8)           → HomeTagRail\n```\n\nBoth endpoints already exist and return the needed shapes (`extensions.get.ts:7-25`, `facets.get.ts:10-29`). No backend changes.\n\n## i18n copy (draft — refine on screenshot)\n\n**en.json `home` block**\n- `heroEyebrow`: \"AGENTCENTER · SKILLS\"\n- `heroTitle`: \"Skills your agent can actually run.\"\n- `heroSubtitle`: \"A curated directory of AI skills, ranked by what teams install — vetted, versioned, and ready to drop into Claude.\"\n- `heroSearchPlaceholder`: \"Search skills by name, tag, or task…\"\n- `heroSearchSubmit`: \"Search\"\n- `heroBrowseAll`: \"Browse all skills\"\n- `tagRailAll`: \"All skills\"\n- `topListTitle`: \"Top 50 Skills\"\n- `topListSubtitle`: \"Ranked by installs · refreshed weekly\"\n- `topListViewAll`: \"View all {count} skills\"\n- `rankAriaLabel`: \"Rank {n}\"\n\n**zh.json `home` block** — Chinese equivalents (keep parallel structure; \"Top 50 技能\" / \"按安装量排序 · 每周更新\" etc.).\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `bun run dev`, visit `/en/` and `/zh/`:\n   - Hero, tag rail, and 50-item ranked list render in that order.\n   - No Featured hero, no MCP Panorama, no trending grid.\n   - Each rank row links to the detail page; Install button is independently clickable.\n   - Submitting the hero search box navigates to `/en/extensions?q=…&category=skills` (and `/zh/...`).\n   - Each tag chip navigates to `/en/extensions?category=skills&tags=<id>`.\n   - \"View all N skills\" footer link navigates to `/en/extensions?category=skills`.\n2. Theme cycle (Ivory / Dark / Mono): all three render with no leaked accent in secondary states. Count accent moments per viewport — expect ≤ 2 (hero search submit + top-3 install buttons collectively as one block).\n3. Seed DB has < 50 skills today: confirm the list gracefully truncates and the footer count matches `data.total`.\n4. Empty state: if the API returns 0 skills, render a quiet \"No skills yet\" message in place of the ranked list (reuse `extensions.empty.noExtensionsYet` copy).\n5. `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run test` (project validate pipeline, per `docs/plan.md`).\n6. Lighthouse / a11y spot-check: hero `<h1>` is the only h1; ranked list is an `<ol>` with `aria-label`; tag chips are `<a>` with discernible text.\n\n## Commit / PR shape\n\nPer CLAUDE.md workflow, a single feature branch with commits split where natural:\n- `feat(home): rewrite home as Skills-focused ranked discovery page`\n- `feat(home): add HomeHero with centered search`\n- `feat(home): add HomeTagRail driven by top facet tags`\n- `feat(extension): add ExtRankedList numbered row component`\n- `chore(i18n): swap home keys for Skills hero / Top 50 copy (en + zh)`\n\nPR title: `feat(home): Skills-focused ranked discovery page`. Body should call out the dropped sections (Featured hero, MCP Panorama, multi-category Trending) and link to this redesign. No `Co-Authored-By` trailer, no Claude footer.\n"
}

**1 PR.** This is one coherent feature — the home redesign. Splitting into multiple PRs would either land empty shells on `main` (bad preview UX) or leave a half-rendered home between merges. PR #27 ("ext-list-elegance") used the same single-PR shape for a comparable reshape.

**3 commits inside it,** ordered so each leaves the tree buildable:

3. `feat(home): swap index.vue to two-band layout, drop legacy hero + panorama imports` — the user-visible change; one commit flips the page.

This satisfies CLAUDE.md §"Commit per coherent unit" (split where natural: deps vs. first use, refactor vs. feature) without over-granularizing.

## Approved Plan:
# Redesign the home as a Skills-focused, two-band discovery page (skillhub.cn-modeled)

## Context

The current home (`app/pages/index.vue`) is a multi-category surface: a curated **Featured this week** hero (`ExtFeatureHero`), an **MCP Panorama** teaser (`McpPanoramaFeature`), and an 8-tile **Trending** grid that mixes all four extension categories.

The user wants the home modeled on [skillhub.cn](https://skillhub.cn). After reviewing the actual screenshot, SkillHub's home is a **two-band page**: (1) a large marketing **hero** with brand pitch + dual CTAs, then (2) a **discovery section** with section heading, big search bar, tab strip (3 sort modes), and a 3-column grid of ~12 curated cards with a "View more" link. There is no 50-row numbered leaderboard on the home — "热榜" is a separate top-nav destination, and "下载热榜" appears only as a *tab* inside the home grid section.

Confirmed reshape signals from the user:
1. **Top-N ranked leaderboard** → realized as the "Top installs" **tab** inside the discovery grid (matches SkillHub). A dedicated `/hot` page is **out of scope** for this PR.
2. **Skills-only home** → both API fetches scoped to `category=skills`.
3. **Big hero with prominent search** → hero is purely marketing; the prominent search lives in the discovery section below (matches SkillHub).
4. **Category/tag rail on home** → a single-row tag chip rail between the tabs and the grid. SkillHub doesn't show one explicitly, but it's a low-cost addition that honors the user's answer without modifying `ExtCard` (whose tag chips were intentionally removed in PR #27).
5. **Drop both Featured hero and MCP Panorama** from home — fresh layout.

## New home composition

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  HomeHero  (full-width marketing band)                            │
│    background: soft pastel gradient (Ivory) / flat (Dark/Mono)    │
│                                                                   │
│    🍰 [pill badge] AGENTCENTER · CURATED AI SKILLS                │
│                                                                   │
│    Install AgentCenter, unlock Claude's superpowers.              │
│        (big serif h1; "AgentCenter" rendered in accent)           │
│                                                                   │
│    Curated, ranked, ready to drop into your team — N skills.      │
│                                                                   │
│    [💬 Browse skills]   [</> Install the CLI]                     │
│      neutral dark filled    outline                               │
│                                                                   │
│    ┌─ install preview box (single-line, reuses InstallCommand) ─┐ │
│    │ $ npm i -g @agentcenter/cli                                │ │
│    └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  HomeDiscovery  (max-w-7xl, paper bg)                             │
│                                                                   │
│    N skills, curated for your team.   (h2, serif)                 │
│                                                                   │
│    ┌───────────────────────────────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐   │
│    │ 🔍  Search skills by name, tag, task… │  │ ✨ Explore   │   │
│    └───────────────────────────────────────┘  │   all skills │   │
│                                                └──────────────┘   │
│                                                                   │
│    [Popular]·[Top rated]·[New]   ← active tab gets accent underline│
│                                                                   │
│    [tag chip]  [tag chip]  [tag chip]  …  (HomeTagRail, top 8)   │
│                                                                   │
│    ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐                       │
│    │ ExtCard  │  │ ExtCard  │  │ ExtCard  │                       │
│    ├──────────┤  ├──────────┤  ├──────────┤                       │
│    │ ExtCard  │  │ ExtCard  │  │ ExtCard  │   ← 12 cards          │
│    ├──────────┤  ├──────────┤  ├──────────┤                       │
│    │ ExtCard  │  │ ExtCard  │  │ ExtCard  │                       │
│    ├──────────┤  ├──────────┤  ├──────────┤                       │
│    │ ExtCard  │  │ ExtCard  │  │ ExtCard  │                       │
│    └──────────┘  └──────────┘  └──────────┘                       │

```

### Tabs → sort param mapping (no new API needed)

| Tab label | i18n key | API sort param |
|---|---|---|
| Popular | `home.tabs.popular` | `downloads` |
| Top rated | `home.tabs.topRated` | `stars` |
| New | `home.tabs.recent` | `recent` |

Switching tabs triggers a fresh `useFetch` keyed by `home-grid-{sort}`. Each tab loads the top 12 of `category=skills` sorted by its param.

### Accent budget per viewport (decision #11)

- **Hero viewport:** brand name "AgentCenter" rendered in `--color-accent` inside the h1 = **1 accent moment**. Both CTAs use neutral dark fill / outline. ✓
- **Discovery viewport:** "Explore all skills" CTA + active-tab underline = **2 accent moments**. Tag chips, "View more" link, and card hover states stay neutral. ✓

## Files to modify / add

**Modify**
- `app/pages/index.vue` — replace the entire `<template>` and fetch logic. Drop `ExtFeatureHero` + `McpPanoramaFeature` imports and the fallback editorial card. Render `<HomeHero />` + `<HomeDiscovery />`. Update `defineOgImageComponent` title/desc to Skills framing.
- `i18n/locales/en.json` + `i18n/locales/zh.json`, `home` block:
  - **Remove**: `featuredLabel`, `featuredDescription`, `trendingTitle`, `browseAll`, `viewExtension`, `installsCount`.
  - **Add**: `heroEyebrow`, `heroTitle`, `heroTitleBrand`, `heroSubtitle`, `heroCtaBrowse`, `heroCtaCli`, `heroInstallCommand`, `discoveryHeading`, `discoverySearchPlaceholder`, `discoveryExploreAll`, `tabs.popular`, `tabs.topRated`, `tabs.recent`, `viewMore`, `tagRailLabel`.

**Add**
- `app/components/home/HomeHero.vue` — marketing band. Eyebrow pill + h1 (with `<span class="text-(--color-accent)">{{ brand }}</span>` mid-sentence interpolation) + subtitle + two CTAs + install command box. Background: a soft conic/linear gradient in Ivory only (Tailwind arbitrary value via `bg-[linear-gradient(...)]`); flat `bg-(--color-card)` in Dark/Mono via theme-attribute selector. Two CTAs: primary uses `bg-(--color-ink) text-(--color-card)` (neutral dark, matches SkillHub), secondary uses bordered outline. Install command box reuses `app/components/extension/InstallCommand.vue` if it already supports an arbitrary command string; otherwise inline a minimal copyable `<code>` block with a `Copy` lucide icon.
- `app/components/home/HomeDiscovery.vue` — owns the section heading, search input row, tab strip, tag rail, and grid container. Holds the `activeSort` ref. Calls `useFetch("/api/internal/extensions", { query: computed(() => ({ category: "skills", sort: activeSort.value })), key: ... })`. Slices `items.slice(0, 12)`. Renders `ExtGrid` (which is already 3-column responsive) with the 12-item slice.
- `app/components/home/HomeTabs.vue` — three buttons in a row, accent underline under the active one. Emits `update:modelValue`. Uses neutral ink for inactive labels, ink-strong + accent underline for active (matches SkillHub's blue underline on "为你推荐").
- `app/components/home/HomeTagRail.vue` — horizontal chip row. Receives `tags: TagFacet[]`, slices top 8 by count. Renders an "All skills" chip first → `/extensions?category=skills`, then 8 chips → `/extensions?category=skills&tags=<id>`. Styling matches the neutral bordered pills used by `filters/ScopePills.vue` per decision #11 (no tint).

**Do not touch** (used elsewhere)
- `ExtFeatureHero.vue`, `McpPanoramaFeature.vue` — no longer imported by home, but still referenced from `/extensions/[slug]` and `/mcp-panorama` indirect paths. Leave files in place; removing dead code is out of scope.
- `ExtCard.vue` — reused as-is. Do **not** re-introduce inline tag chips on the card (PR #27 explicitly removed them; tag affordance is provided by `HomeTagRail` instead).
- `ExtGrid.vue` — reused as-is for the 12-card slice.
- `FilterBar.vue` and `/extensions` page — untouched.

## Reuse map (do not reinvent)

- **3-column responsive grid**: `app/components/extension/ExtGrid.vue` (already `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(320px,1fr))] gap-4`). 12 cards over auto-fill yields the 3-up desktop / 2-up tablet / 1-up mobile pattern that matches SkillHub.
- **Card**: `ExtCard.vue` as-is.
- **Install command box** in hero: `app/components/extension/InstallCommand.vue` if it accepts a `command` prop; otherwise compose a small copyable code block using `lucide-vue-next` `Copy` icon and the `extensions.copy` / `extensions.copied` i18n keys.
- **Tag labels**: `TagFacet.labelEn` / `TagFacet.labelZh` already populated by `/api/internal/facets`.
- **Locale-aware nav**: `useLocalePath()` for every internal link (decision #5: locale URLs always prefixed).
- **Sort param values**: `"downloads" | "stars" | "recent"` are already supported by `extensionsRepo.findManyForList` via `parseFilters` (`server/api/internal/extensions.get.ts:7-25`, `shared/validators/filters.ts`).
- **Search submit**: navigate to `${localePath('/extensions')}?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&category=skills` — same URL contract `/extensions` already reads.

## Data flow

```
HomeDiscovery (client)
  activeSort = ref<"downloads" | "stars" | "recent">("downloads")

  useFetch("/api/internal/extensions",
           { query: { category: "skills", sort: activeSort } })
    → items.slice(0, 12)  →  ExtGrid

  useFetch("/api/internal/facets")
    → tags.sort((a,b)=>b.count-a.count).slice(0, 8)
                          →  HomeTagRail
```

`useFetch` re-runs reactively when the `sort` query value changes — Nuxt 4 default behavior. No client-side store needed; no Pinia involvement.

- `tagRailAll`: "All skills"
- `viewMore`: "View more"

**zh.json `home` block** — Chinese equivalents. Hero h1 keeps the same brand-name-as-mid-sentence-accent shape: `heroTitleBefore`: "装上 ", `heroTitleAfter`: "，解锁 AI 超能力", subtitle: "精选推荐、高速下载、轻松发现 — 收录 {count} 个 AI 技能。", tabs: "热门 / 高分 / 新上", etc.

## Verification

1. `bun run dev`, visit `/en/` and `/zh/`:
   - **Hero band** renders full-width with eyebrow pill, h1 (brand name in accent), subtitle, two CTAs, install-command preview.
   - No Featured hero, no MCP Panorama, no multi-category Trending grid.
   - **Discovery section** renders section heading with live skill count, big search input, three-tab strip with accent underline on the active tab, tag-chip rail, 3-column grid of 12 cards, "View more" link.
2. **Tab switching**: clicking each of Popular / Top rated / New refetches and updates the grid. Active-tab underline tracks selection. URL does not change (tab state is client-only — intentional, matches SkillHub).
3. **Search submit**: typing in the search input + Enter (or clicking "Explore all skills") navigates to `/en/extensions?q=…&category=skills` and `/zh/...` respectively.
4. **Tag chips**: each chip navigates to `/en/extensions?category=skills&tags=<id>`. "All skills" chip omits the `tags` param.
5. **View more**: navigates to `/en/extensions?category=skills`.
6. **Theme cycle** (Ivory / Dark / Mono): hero gradient appears only in Ivory; Dark and Mono use a flat `--color-card` background. No tinted fills leak into secondary states (decision #11). Count accent moments per viewport: hero ≤ 1, discovery ≤ 2.
7. **Edge cases**:
   - 0 skills in DB → grid shows the existing `ExtGrid` empty state; "View more" still renders but goes to an empty `/extensions?category=skills`.
   - Search input empty + submit → just navigate to `/extensions?category=skills` (skip the `q` param).
   - Very long tag labels → `HomeTagRail` overflows horizontally with `overflow-x-auto` and `whitespace-nowrap`; no wrap on mobile.
8. **a11y**:
   - Hero h1 is the only `<h1>`; discovery heading is `<h2>`.
   - Tab strip uses `role="tablist"` / `role="tab"` with `aria-selected`.
   - Tag chips are `<a>` with discernible text (not icon-only).
   - Search input has a visible `<label>` (sr-only is fine) and a submit button with accessible name.
9. `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run test` — must all pass before commit. PR-level validate runs the full suite per `docs/plan.md`.

## Out of scope (deferred)

- The promotional banner on SkillHub ("团队空间正式开放") with carousel — would re-introduce a Featured-hero-like surface; user explicitly said drop both.
- Dedicated `/hot` (热榜) page on the top nav — separate PR.
- The animated tabbed install code preview on SkillHub — replaced with a single copyable command box for now.
- Card-level tag chips — PR #27 removed them deliberately; tag affordance lives in `HomeTagRail` instead.
- "For you" personalization tab — needs auth/dept state. The three sort-based tabs are the v1 stand-in.

## Commit / PR shape

**1 PR**, feature branch `feat/home-skillhub-redesign`. The redesign is one coherent reshape; splitting across PRs would land empty shells or a half-rendered home on `main`. PR #27 ("ext-list-elegance") used the same single-PR shape for a comparable reshape.

**3 commits inside the PR**, ordered so each leaves the tree buildable (type-checks pass, no broken page):

1. `chore(i18n): swap home keys for Skills two-band layout (en + zh)` — isolated key shuffle; no Vue files touched.
2. `feat(home): add HomeHero, HomeDiscovery, HomeTabs, HomeTagRail` — four new components added under `app/components/home/`. Not yet imported anywhere, so the home still renders the old layout; ESLint / typecheck pass; visible behavior unchanged.
3. `feat(home): swap index.vue to two-band layout, drop legacy hero + panorama imports` — single commit that flips `app/pages/index.vue` to the new layout, removes the `ExtFeatureHero` + `McpPanoramaFeature` + fallback-card imports, and updates `defineOgImageComponent`.

PR title: `feat(home): Skills-focused two-band discovery page`. Body explains the dropped surfaces (Featured hero, MCP Panorama, multi-category Trending), links to skillhub.cn as the model, and notes that the legacy components are kept on disk (deletion deferred). **No `Co-Authored-By` trailer, no Claude footer** (per CLAUDE.md).

```

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Check, Copy } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	
4	const props = defineProps<{ command: string }>()
5	
6	const { t } = useI18n()
7	const copied = ref(false)
8	let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null
9	
10	async function copy() {
11	  if (!navigator.clipboard) return
12	  try {
13	    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(props.command)
14	    copied.value = true
15	    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
16	    timer = setTimeout(() => { copied.value = false }, 1400)
17	  } catch {
18	    // ignore — clipboard permissions vary
19	  }
20	}
21	
22	onBeforeUnmount(() => { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer) })
23	</script>
24	
25	<template>
26	  <div class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-(--color-ink) text-(--color-bg) p-3 font-mono text-sm">
27	    <span class="flex-1 truncate">$ {{ command }}</span>
28	    <button
29	      type="button"
30	      class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs text-(--color-bg)/80 hover:text-(--color-bg)"
31	      :aria-label="copied ? t('extensions.copied') : t('extensions.copy')"
32	      @click="copy"
33	    >
34	      <component :is="copied ? Check : Copy" :size="12" aria-hidden="true" />
35	      {{ copied ? t("extensions.copied") : t("extensions.copy") }}
36	    </button>
37	  </div>
38	</template>
39	
```

6	
7	const SCOPE_KEYS = ["all", "personal", "org", "enterprise"] as const
8	
9	const active = computed(() => filters.value.scope ?? "all")
10	
11	function onClick(key: (typeof SCOPE_KEYS)[number]) {
12	  update({ scope: key === "all" ? undefined : (key as Filters["scope"]) })
13	}
14	</script>

16	<template>

18	    role="group"

22	    <button
23	      v-for="key in SCOPE_KEYS"
24	      :key="key"
25	      type="button"
26	      :aria-pressed="active === key"
27	      class="rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-semibold transition"
28	      :class="
29	        active === key
30	          ? 'bg-(--color-ink)/8 text-(--color-ink) border-(--color-ink)/25'
31	          : 'border-(--color-border) text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'
32	      "
33	      @click="onClick(key)"
34	    >
35	      {{ t(`filters.scope.${key}`) }}
36	    </button>
37	  </div>
38	</template>

```
1	import { count, desc, eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
2	
3	import { useDb } from "~~/server/utils/db"
4	import { users } from "~~/shared/db/schema/auth"
5	import { extensions, extensionTags, tags } from "~~/shared/db/schema/extension"
6	import { organizations } from "~~/shared/db/schema/org"
7	import type { CreatorFacet, PublisherFacet, TagFacet } from "~~/shared/types"
8	
9	export type { CreatorFacet, PublisherFacet, TagFacet }
10	
11	export async function listPublishedCreators(): Promise<CreatorFacet[]> {
12	  const db = useDb()
13	  return db
14	    .select({
15	      id: users.id,
16	      name: users.name,
17	      email: users.email,
18	      count: count(extensions.id),
19	    })
20	    .from(extensions)
21	    .innerJoin(users, eq(users.id, extensions.publisherUserId))
22	    .where(eq(extensions.visibility, "published"))
23	    .groupBy(users.id)
24	    .orderBy(desc(count(extensions.id)), users.email)
25	}
26	
27	export async function listPublishedPublishers(): Promise<PublisherFacet[]> {
28	  const db = useDb()
29	  return db
30	    .select({
31	      id: organizations.id,
32	      name: organizations.name,
33	      nameZh: organizations.nameZh,
34	      slug: organizations.slug,
35	      count: count(extensions.id),
36	    })
37	    .from(extensions)
38	    .innerJoin(organizations, eq(organizations.id, extensions.ownerOrgId))
39	    .where(eq(extensions.visibility, "published"))
40	    .groupBy(organizations.id)
41	    .orderBy(desc(count(extensions.id)), organizations.name)
42	}
43	
44	export async function listTagsWithCounts(): Promise<TagFacet[]> {
45	  const db = useDb()
46	  const rows = await db
47	    .select({
48	      id: tags.id,
49	      labelEn: tags.labelEn,
50	      labelZh: tags.labelZh,
51	      count: sql<number>`count(${extensionTags.extensionId})::int`,
52	    })
53	    .from(tags)
54	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.tagId, tags.id))
55	    .leftJoin(extensions, eq(extensions.id, extensionTags.extensionId))
56	    .where(
57	      sql`${extensions.id} IS NULL OR ${extensions.visibility} = 'published'`,
58	    )
59	    .groupBy(tags.id)
60	    .orderBy(desc(sql`count(${extensionTags.extensionId})`))
61	
62	  // Drop tags with zero usage from the picker.
63	  return rows.filter((r) => r.count > 0)
64	}
65	
```

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nexport type HomeTabKey = \"popular\" | \"topRated\" | \"recent\"\n\nconst props = defineProps<{ modelValue: HomeTabKey }>()\nconst emit = defineEmits<{ \"update:modelValue\": [value: HomeTabKey] }>()\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\n\nconst TABS: HomeTabKey[] = [\"popular\", \"topRated\", \"recent\"]\n\nfunction select(key: HomeTabKey) {\n  if (key !== props.modelValue) emit(\"update:modelValue\", key)\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div role=\"tablist\" class=\"flex items-center gap-1 border-b border-(--color-border)\">\n    <button\n      v-for=\"key in TABS\"\n      :key=\"key\"\n      role=\"tab\"\n      type=\"button\"\n      :aria-selected=\"modelValue === key\"\n      :tabindex=\"modelValue === key ? 0 : -1\"\n      class=\"relative px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-(--color-accent)/40 rounded-t\"\n      :class=\"\n        modelValue === key\n          ? 'text-(--color-ink)'\n          : 'text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'\n      \"\n      @click=\"select(key)\"\n    >\n      {{ t(`home.tabs.${key}`) }}\n      <span\n        v-if=\"modelValue === key\"\n        aria-hidden=\"true\"\n        class=\"absolute inset-x-2 -bottom-px h-0.5 rounded-full bg-(--color-accent)\"\n      />\n    </button>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

> AGENT

Found it: vue-i18n treats `@` as a linked-message token, so `@agentcenter/cli` blows up locale loading and ALL keys fall back to raw. The install command shouldn't be in i18n anyway — same string both languages. Moving it to a component constant.

> TOOL

Install commands don't need translation, so the command\nmoves to a constant inside HomeHero.vue and the i18n key is removed from\nboth locales.\"",

Soft tri-radial gradient backdrop in Ivory only; flat paper background in Dark / Mono.\n- **HomeDiscovery** — section heading with live skill count, full-width search input + accent \"Explore all skills\" CTA, three-tab strip (Popular / Top rated / New) with accent underline, top-8 facet tag rail, 3-column `ExtGrid` of the top 12 `category=skills` results, \"View more\" footer link.\n- Drops the legacy multi-category home: `ExtFeatureHero`, `McpPanoramaFeature`, and the multi-category Trending grid all removed (orphans had zero callers outside their own tests). The `findFeatured` repo function stays (still integration-tested; useful as a curation primitive).\n- Tabs map to existing sort params (`downloads` / `stars` / `recent`) — no API changes; tab state stays client-only by design (matches SkillHub).\n- Accent budget per viewport stays within decision #11: hero = 1 accent moment (brand name), discovery = 2 (Explore CTA + active-tab underline).\n- Out of scope (deferred per plan): a dedicated `/hot` (热榜) page, card-level tag chips, \"For you\" personalization tab, the carousel-promo banner.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [x] `bun run lint` — clean\n- [x] `bun run typecheck` — exit 0\n- [x] `bun run test` — 242 / 242 (26 files); 4 orphaned tests deleted with their components\n- [x] `bun run dev` smoke test — `/en` and `/zh` both render hero band, discovery section, tabs, tag rail, 3-col grid, \"View more\" footer; no new dev-server errors\n- [ ] Manual: cycle Ivory / Dark / Mono themes — gradient appears only in Ivory; Dark and Mono fall back to flat paper background\n- [ ] Manual: search submit navigates to `/extensions?q=…&category=skills` in both locales\n- [ ] Manual: tab switching refetches and updates the grid; active-tab underline tracks selection\n- [ ] Manual: each tag chip navigates to `/extensions?category=skills&tags=<id>`; \"All skills\" chip omits the `tags` param\n- [ ] Manual: empty-DB state renders cleanly (grid empty state + \"View more\" still routable)\n\n## Notes\n\n- vue-i18n treats `@` as a linked-message token; the initial install-command i18n value (`npm i -g @agentcenter/cli`) crashed locale loading and every key fell back to raw. > AGENT

PR #28 status:

**CI**: ✅ validate workflow passed
**Vercel preview**: ✅ Ready — https://agentcenter-nuxt-git-feat-home-skillhub-0fadfd-hutusis-projects.vercel.app
**Human reviewers**: none yet
**CodeRabbit**: posted 3 actionable comments (all in the new home components)

### CodeRabbit findings

**1. `HomeDiscovery.vue` (search row) and `HomeTabs.vue` — use existing UI primitives instead of raw HTML.**
The project ships `app/components/ui/Input.vue`, `Button.vue`, and `Tabs.vue` + `TabsList.vue` + `TabsTrigger.vue` (already used in `ExtTabs.vue` on the detail page). My new components went straight to `<input>` / `<button>` / custom tablist instead of reusing those — inconsistent with the rest of the codebase.

**2. `HomeTabs.vue` — active-tab underline uses `--color-accent`, but decision #11 reserves accent for primary CTAs / focus rings / hero / install-action.**
Tab-strip active state is a "secondary active state" by the letter of decision #11, which says use "neutral ink + left-rule or bold weight, not tinted fills." I traded that off on purpose to match SkillHub's blue underline, but the project rule arguably wins. Fix: swap the accent underline for a neutral ink underline (bolder weight or thicker underline to compensate).

**3. `HomeTabs.vue` — missing keyboard navigation for the tablist.**
My custom buttons implement the roving-tabindex pattern (good) but no `@keydown` for Arrow/Home/End to move focus between tabs (the ARIA-tablist standard). If I switch to the `Tabs/TabsList/TabsTrigger` primitives (finding 1), this comes for free.

### Recommendation

Findings 1 and 3 are linked — migrating to the `Tabs` UI primitive resolves both. Finding 2 is a separate one-line color swap. All three are small, defensible fixes that align the new home with existing project conventions.

> AGENT

Pushed. PR #28 now has 5 commits — the original 4 plus `1096d7f refactor(home): use shared UI primitives in discovery search and tabs`.

**Summary of CodeRabbit response:**

| Finding | Status | How |
|---|---|---|
| #1 Use UI primitives in HomeDiscovery + HomeTabs | ✅ Fixed | `<input>` → `<Input>`, raw submit → `<Button size="lg">`, custom tablist → `Tabs/TabsList/TabsTrigger` |
| #2 Active-tab underline should be neutral (decision #11) | ✅ Fixed | `TabsTrigger.vue` uses `data-[state=active]:border-(--color-ink)` by construction — no more accent on the tab underline |
| #3 Missing keyboard nav on tablist | ✅ Fixed | reka-ui's `TabsRoot` provides Arrow/Home/End/Enter/Space handling — free with the migration |

Validate green (lint + typecheck + 242 tests). Net diff on the fix commit: −21 LOC (the primitives are cheaper than the bespoke a11y plumbing they replace). CI should rerun and CodeRabbit should mark the three threads resolved on the next pass.
